## 10. Complete development and sales timeline

{@body const plan=[{week:"Week 1",title:"Research and demo",tasks:["Confirm hospital identity and existing website","Ask permission before using hospital branding","Build a fictional-content homepage demo","Deploy demo to Cloudflare Pages"]},{week:"Week 2",title:"Client meeting and proposal",tasks:["Show the demo to hospital management","Ask about existing website and patient inquiries","Confirm pages, languages, budget and content ownership","Send a written proposal and payment milestones"]},{week:"Week 3",title:"Development after approval",tasks:["Receive advance payment and approved content","Build responsive pages and contact features","Test mobile, desktop, links and accessibility","Collect client feedback"]},{week:"Week 4",title:"Launch and handover",tasks:["Complete revisions and acceptance testing","Connect hospital-owned domain and hosting","Publish after final approval","Deliver documentation and maintenance agreement"]}]}
{@body const [completed,setCompleted]=DIL.useState({})}
<box border radius="xl" padding={3} gap={3}>
  <row justify="between" align="center">
    **4-week project checklist**
    <text color="secondary" size="xs">{Object.values(completed).filter(Boolean).length}/16 tasks</text>
  </row>
  {#each plan as p,i}
    <box gap={2} key={p.week}>
      <text weight="medium">{p.week} — {p.title}</text>
      {#each p.tasks as task,j}
        <checkbox key={`${i}-${j}`} checked={!!completed[`${i}-${j}`]} onChange={v=>setCompleted(prev=>({...prev,[`${i}-${j}`]:v}))}>{task}</checkbox>
      {/each}
    </box>
    {#if i!==3}<divider color="subtle"/>{/if}
  {/each}
</box>

## 11. Your quotation and profit calculation

Here is an example of how much you could charge for a basic website.

{@body const [price,setPrice]=DIL.useState(10000)}
{@body const [domain,setDomain]=DIL.useState(1000)}
{@body const [other,setOther]=DIL.useState(500)}
{@body const [labor,setLabor]=DIL.useState(30)}
{@body const margin=price-domain-other}
<box border radius="xl" padding={3} gap={3}>
  <box gap={1}>
    <row justify="between"><label>Client development price</label>**?{price.toLocaleString("en-IN")}**</row>
    <slider min={5000} max={30000} step={1000} value={price} onChange={setPrice}/>
  </box>
  <box gap={1}>
    <row justify="between"><label>Domain cost paid by you</label>**?{domain.toLocaleString("en-IN")}**</row>
    <slider min={0} max={2500} step={100} value={domain} onChange={setDomain}/>
  </box>
  <box gap={1}>
    <row justify="between"><label>Other project expenses</label>**?{other.toLocaleString("en-IN")}**</row>
    <slider min={0} max={5000} step={250} value={other} onChange={setOther}/>
  </box>
  <box gap={1}>
    <row justify="between"><label>Your development hours</label>**{labor} hours**</row>
    <slider min={10} max={100} step={5} value={labor} onChange={setLabor}/>
  </box>
  <divider color="subtle"/>
  <grid columns={2} gap={3}>
    <grid-item>
      <text color="secondary" size="xs">Before your labor and taxes</text>
      <title size="xl" color="success">?{margin.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</title>
    </grid-item>
    <grid-item>
      <text color="secondary" size="xs">Effective hourly amount</text>
      <title size="xl">?{Math.round(margin/labor).toLocaleString("en-IN")}</title>
    </grid-item>
  </grid>
  <caption>This is contribution after the selected expenses, not net profit. It excludes your labor cost, taxes, revisions beyond scope and future support.</caption>
</box>

### Recommended payment terms

For a ?10,000 website:

| Milestone | Payment |
|---|---:|
| 40% advance after agreement | ?4,000 |
| 40% after approved development preview | ?4,000 |
| 20% before final handover | ?2,000 |
| **Total** | **?10,000** |

The agreement should specify the number of pages, revisions, content responsibilities, timeline, third-party expenses, ownership, cancellation terms, and what maintenance includes.

## 12. Free add-ons that increase your offer's value

You can include several useful features without significantly increasing your development costs.

| Add-on | Benefit |
|---|---|
| Google Maps directions | Patients find the hospital |
| Click-to-call button | One-tap phone contact |
| WhatsApp contact link | Easy general inquiries |
| Google Business Profile updates | Accurate Search and Maps information |
| Basic SEO | Better page structure and search visibility |
| SSL certificate | Secure website connection |
| Google Search Console | Monitor search indexing |
| Basic web analytics | Measure website traffic |
| QR code for website | Place on brochures or reception desk |

Google Business Profile itself is free, so you can charge for your time setting it up or improving it, but not describe it as a paid Google listing. <Cite ref="turn815651search0"/>

## 13. What to ask during your first hospital meeting

{@body const questions=["Do you already have a website or someone managing it?","Who manages your Google Business Profile?","Which hospital services and doctors should be displayed?","What languages should the website support?","Do you want phone inquiries or online appointment requests?","Who will provide photographs, text and approvals?","Who will update the website after launch?","What budget and launch date would work for you?"]}
{@body const [answers,setAnswers]=DIL.useState({})}
<box border radius="xl" padding={3} gap={2}>
  **Client discovery checklist**
  {#each questions as q,i}
    <checkbox key={i} checked={!!answers[i]} onChange={v=>setAnswers(p=>({...p,[i]:v}))}>{q}</checkbox>
  {/each}
  <button block color="primary" variant="outline" onClick={()=>GenUI.copy(questions.map((q,i)=>`${i+1}. ${q}`).join("\n"))}>
    <icon name="copy" inline/> Copy meeting questions
  </button>
</box>

## Final recommendation — Your first real freelance project

For Mahavir Hospital, I would prepare a **free demonstration homepage**, not a complete free website.

Then pitch a ?10,000–?15,000 basic hospital website with optional annual maintenance. Keep the initial scope small, use Cloudflare Pages for hosting, and avoid storing patient information.

That demo will also become a reusable foundation for approaching other clinics, academies and businesses around Shirpur.

**Our next concrete step should be building the Mahavir Hospital demo homepage in React**, with responsive desktop/mobile layouts, Marathi and English content support, and placeholder hospital information. Once it is working, we can deploy it free and prepare your first client presentation.

# Mahaveer Hospital, Shirpur — Complete Freelance Project &amp; Business Proposal

Website • Appointment Booking • Hospital Administration • Hosting • Pricing • Deployment • Client Acquisition

Our objective is to approach Mahaveer Hospital in Shirpur with a professional proposal that explains **how a website or web application could help the hospital, what it would cost, and why they should hire you**\.

I recommend starting with a small, practical solution rather than immediately trying to sell an expensive hospital management system.

## 1\. What exactly are we selling?

A hospital may already receive patients through referrals, phone calls and walk-ins. We need to show how a website could make information easier to find and reduce repetitive administrative work.

?image_group?{"query":["modern Indian hospital professional website desktop homepage clean white blue medical design appointment button"],"aspect_ratio":"5:4","layout":"carousel","num_per_query":1}?

**Phase 1 — Hospital Website**

Hospital information, doctors, departments, services, location, visiting hours, phone and WhatsApp contact.

Small project • Quick delivery

---

?image_group?{"query":["hospital doctor appointment booking calendar web app clean modern user interface"],"aspect_ratio":"5:4","layout":"carousel","num_per_query":1}?

**Phase 2 — Appointment Application**

Appointment requests, staff dashboard, doctor availability and booking confirmation.

Medium project • Recurring maintenance opportunity

---

?image_group?{"query":["hospital management software dashboard patient appointment statistics modern desktop application"],"aspect_ratio":"5:4","layout":"carousel","num_per_query":1}?

**Phase 3 — Hospital Management System**

Patient registration, role-based staff access, operational reporting and integrations.

Large project • Requires detailed discovery and stronger security

**My recommendation\:** Pitch Phase 1 first, demonstrate Phase 2, and only propose Phase 3 if the hospital confirms it needs those capabilities.

## 2\. Hospital research — what we know

## ?entity?["turn548371business0","Mahavir Hospital Critical Care Center"]?

Dwarkamai Lodge, Old, Main Road, near Bus stand, Shirpur, Varvade, Shirpur-Warwade, Maharashtra 425405, India

Hospital and critical care facility

Public business listing available

Public listings and reviews describe the hospital as providing medical and critical-care services, with references to emergency availability and Dr. Darshan Rakhecha. However, its complete department list, appointment process and official website have not been independently confirmed.

**Opportunity\:** A dedicated website could make verified hospital information easier for patients to find. But before selling the project, we must ask whether the hospital already has a website, who maintains its Google listing, and how patients currently contact it.

## 3\. What will convince the hospital?

Don't open your pitch with "I can build a website." Start with the problems a website might solve.

| Hospital concern | What your solution offers |
|--------|--------|
| Patients call for basic information | Services, hours and location online |
| New patients struggle to find the hospital | Google Maps and clear directions |
| Staff answer repeated appointment questions | Appointment inquiry form |
| Doctors' availability is unclear | Verified doctor and OPD schedules |
| Hospital information changes | Easy-to-update content |
| Management wants a professional online presence | Mobile-friendly branded website |

We cannot promise a specific increase in patient numbers or Google rankings. We can offer a more accessible, measurable online experience.

These are **proposed quotes for your freelance business**, not confirmed local market rates.

- Staff login and dashboard

- Testing, deployment and staff training

For a complete hospital management system involving patient records, billing, clinical workflows or integrations, **don't quote a fixed amount yet**\. It requires a separate requirements document, privacy assessment, security architecture and acceptance plan.

## 5\. Technology stack

For the first website, use the simplest reliable technology.

| Layer | Recommended technology |
|--------|--------|
| UI | React + Tailwind CSS |
| Programming | JavaScript |
| Routing | React Router |
| Images | Optimized WebP |
| Maps | Google Maps link or embed |
| Contact | Phone / WhatsApp links |
| Version control | GitHub private repository |
| Hosting | Cloudflare Pages |
| Domain | Hospital-owned `.in` or `.com` |
| HTTPS | Hosting-managed SSL |

**Why no .NET backend initially?** A brochure website doesn't need a server or database. Adding one creates unnecessary cost and maintenance.

For Phase 2, we can use ASP.NET Core Web API, SQL Server or PostgreSQL, and a secure authentication system.

## 6\. Free hosting, deployment and recurring costs

For a basic hospital website, you can genuinely keep the hosting cost close to zero. A real appointment application is different: it needs reliable hosting, secure storage and operational support.

| Service | Free option | My recommendation |
|--------|--------|--------|
| Source control | GitHub | Free private repository |
| Static hosting | Cloudflare Pages | Recommended |
| SSL certificate | Cloudflare | Free |
| Domain name | Usually paid | Hospital-owned domain |
| Google Maps | Maps link | Free link |
| WhatsApp | Click-to-chat | Free basic link |
| Google Business Profile | Free | Include in service |
| Analytics | Cloudflare Web Analytics | Free basic analytics |
| Database | Supabase Free | Prototype only |
| Business email | Limited free options | Budget separately |

Cloudflare Pages currently supports 500 builds per month on its free plan, custom domains and automatic deployment from GitHub. ?cite?turn548371search3?turn815651search4?turn815651search6?

Supabase offers a free database tier, but free projects can pause after inactivity, so I wouldn't rely on it for a live hospital appointment system. ?cite?turn548371search0?

### Estimated annual operating costs

| Item | Basic website | Appointment app |
|--------|--------|--------|
| Domain | ?800–?1,500 | ?800–?1,500 |
| Hosting | ?0 | ?6,000–?24,000 |
| SSL | ?0 | ?0–?2,000 |
| Backups / monitoring | ?0–?1,000 | ?2,000–?8,000 |
| Maintenance | ?3,000–?6,000 | ?12,000–?30,000 |
| **Estimated annual total** | **?3,800–?8,500** | **?20,800–?66,500** |

These are planning allowances, not provider quotations. Actual costs depend on usage, reliability requirements, taxes and support scope. SMS, WhatsApp API messaging, paid email, and major feature changes are excluded.

For the basic website, **you could charge ?10,000 for development and ?4,000 per year for maintenance**, with the domain billed separately.

## 7\. How to deploy the hospital website for free

1. **Create the project locally**
   
   Use React and Vite in VS Code.
   
   ```bash
   npm create vite@latest mahavir-demo -- --template react
   cd mahavir-demo
   npm install
   npm run dev
   ```
2. **Build and test the website**
   
   Implement responsive pages, navigation, contact links, accessibility and SEO metadata.
   
   ```bash
   npm run build
   ```
3. **Push to a private GitHub repository**
   
   Use a repository such as `mahavir-hospital-demo`\. Keep credentials and private information out of Git.
4. **Connect Cloudflare Pages**
   
   Open ?url?Cloudflare Dashboard?https://dash.cloudflare.com/? ? Workers &amp; Pages ? Create application ? Connect GitHub repository.
   
   For Vite, use build command `npm run build` and output directory `dist`\.
5. **Publish the demo**
   
   Cloudflare provides a temporary `pages.dev` address. Use that for your sales presentation.
   
   Don't present the demo as the hospital's official website.
6. **Connect the hospital's domain after approval**
   
   In Cloudflare Pages, open Custom domains and follow the DNS instructions. The hospital should own the domain and hosting account.

Cloudflare supports GitHub-triggered deployments, so later website updates can go live automatically after approved code is pushed. ?cite?turn815651search8?

## 9\. Security and privacy requirements

This is especially important because the customer is a hospital.

For Phase 1, I recommend **not collecting medical information at all**\. Patients can call the hospital directly, and a basic inquiry form can be avoided initially.

If Phase 2 includes appointments or patient details, we must add\:

- Explicit privacy notices and appropriate consent or other lawful processing basis
- HTTPS, secure authentication and role-based authorization
- Minimal personal data collection and defined retention periods
- Encrypted storage and protected backups
- Access logs, monitoring and recovery procedures
- Testing and documented incident-response processes
- Clear data ownership and hosting agreements

India's DPDP Act and Rules have phased implementation provisions, so the exact obligations and effective dates should be reviewed before launching a patient-data system. ?cite?turn815651search2?turn815651search10?

For your first paid project, keeping medical records out of scope is the safer and more manageable choice.

## 12\. Free add-ons that increase your offer's value

You can include several useful features without significantly increasing your development costs.

| Add-on | Benefit |
|--------|--------|
| Google Maps directions | Patients find the hospital |

| WhatsApp contact link | Easy general inquiries |
| Google Business Profile updates | Accurate Search and Maps information |
| Basic SEO | Better page structure and search visibility |
| SSL certificate | Secure website connection |
| Google Search Console | Monitor search indexing |
| Basic web analytics | Measure website traffic |
| QR code for website | Place on brochures or reception desk |

Google Business Profile itself is free, so you can charge for your time setting it up or improving it, but not describe it as a paid Google listing. ?cite?turn815651search0?

## Final recommendation — Your first real freelance project

For Mahavir Hospital, I would prepare a **free demonstration homepage**, not a complete free website.

Then pitch a ?10,000–?15,000 basic hospital website with optional annual maintenance. Keep the initial scope small, use Cloudflare Pages for hosting, and avoid storing patient information.

That demo will also become a reusable foundation for approaching other clinics, academies and businesses around Shirpur.

**Our next concrete step should be building the Mahavir Hospital demo homepage in React**, with responsive desktop/mobile layouts, Marathi and English content support, and placeholder hospital information. Once it is working, we can deploy it free and prepare your first client presentation.

# Mahaveer Hospital, Shirpur — Complete Freelance Project & Business Proposal

<text color="secondary" size="sm">Website • Appointment Booking • Hospital Administration • Hosting • Pricing • Deployment • Client Acquisition</text>

Our objective is to approach Mahaveer Hospital in Shirpur with a professional proposal that explains **how a website or web application could help the hospital, what it would cost, and why they should hire you**.

I recommend starting with a small, practical solution rather than immediately trying to sell an expensive hospital management system.

## 1. What exactly are we selling?

A hospital may already receive patients through referrals, phone calls and walk-ins. We need to show how a website could make information easier to find and reduce repetitive administrative work.

<box gap={3}>
  <row align="start" gap={3}>
    <AsyncImage query="modern Indian hospital professional website desktop homepage clean white blue medical design appointment button" aspectRatio="5:4" maxWidth="144px"/>
    <box flex="1" gap={1}>
      **Phase 1 — Hospital Website**

      Hospital information, doctors, departments, services, location, visiting hours, phone and WhatsApp contact.

      <text color="secondary" size="xs">Small project • Quick delivery</text>
    </box>
  </row>
  <divider color="subtle"/>
  <row align="start" gap={3}>
    <AsyncImage query="hospital doctor appointment booking calendar web app clean modern user interface" aspectRatio="5:4" maxWidth="144px"/>
    <box flex="1" gap={1}>
      **Phase 2 — Appointment Application**

      Appointment requests, staff dashboard, doctor availability and booking confirmation.

      <text color="secondary" size="xs">Medium project • Recurring maintenance opportunity</text>
    </box>
  </row>
  <divider color="subtle"/>
  <row align="start" gap={3}>
    <AsyncImage query="hospital management software dashboard patient appointment statistics modern desktop application" aspectRatio="5:4" maxWidth="144px"/>
    <box flex="1" gap={1}>
      **Phase 3 — Hospital Management System**

      Patient registration, role-based staff access, operational reporting and integrations.

      <text color="secondary" size="xs">Large project • Requires detailed discovery and stronger security</text>
    </box>
  </row>
</box>

**My recommendation:** Pitch Phase 1 first, demonstrate Phase 2, and only propose Phase 3 if the hospital confirms it needs those capabilities.

## 2. Hospital research — what we know

<row align="start" gap={3}>
  <box flex="1" gap={1}>
    <title size="lg"><Entity category="business" ref="turn548371business0" value={turn548371business0.name}/></title>
    <text color="secondary" size="sm">{turn548371business0.address}</text>
    <text size="sm">Hospital and critical care facility</text>
    <text size="sm">Public business listing available</text>
  </box>
</row>

Public listings and reviews describe the hospital as providing medical and critical-care services, with references to emergency availability and Dr. Darshan Rakhecha. However, its complete department list, appointment process and official website have not been independently confirmed.

**Opportunity:** A dedicated website could make verified hospital information easier for patients to find. But before selling the project, we must ask whether the hospital already has a website, who maintains its Google listing, and how patients currently contact it.

## 3. What will convince the hospital?

Don't open your pitch with "I can build a website." Start with the problems a website might solve.

| Hospital concern | What your solution offers |
|---|---|
| Patients call for basic information | Services, hours and location online |
| New patients struggle to find the hospital | Google Maps and clear directions |
| Staff answer repeated appointment questions | Appointment inquiry form |
| Doctors' availability is unclear | Verified doctor and OPD schedules |
| Hospital information changes | Easy-to-update content |
| Management wants a professional online presence | Mobile-friendly branded website |

We cannot promise a specific increase in patient numbers or Google rankings. We can offer a more accessible, measurable online experience.

For a complete hospital management system involving patient records, billing, clinical workflows or integrations, **don't quote a fixed amount yet**. It requires a separate requirements document, privacy assessment, security architecture and acceptance plan.

## 5. Technology stack

For the first website, use the simplest reliable technology.

| Layer | Recommended technology |
|---|---|
| UI | React + Tailwind CSS |
| Programming | JavaScript |
| Routing | React Router |
| Images | Optimized WebP |
| Maps | Google Maps link or embed |
| Contact | Phone / WhatsApp links |
| Version control | GitHub private repository |
| Hosting | Cloudflare Pages |
| Domain | Hospital-owned `.in` or `.com` |
| HTTPS | Hosting-managed SSL |

**Why no .NET backend initially?** A brochure website doesn't need a server or database. Adding one creates unnecessary cost and maintenance.

For Phase 2, we can use ASP.NET Core Web API, SQL Server or PostgreSQL, and a secure authentication system.

## 6. Free hosting, deployment and recurring costs

For a basic hospital website, you can genuinely keep the hosting cost close to zero. A real appointment application is different: it needs reliable hosting, secure storage and operational support.

| Service | Free option | My recommendation |
|---|---|---|
| Source control | GitHub | Free private repository |
| Static hosting | Cloudflare Pages | Recommended |
| SSL certificate | Cloudflare | Free |
| Domain name | Usually paid | Hospital-owned domain |
| Google Maps | Maps link | Free link |
| WhatsApp | Click-to-chat | Free basic link |
| Google Business Profile | Free | Include in service |
| Analytics | Cloudflare Web Analytics | Free basic analytics |
| Database | Supabase Free | Prototype only |
| Business email | Limited free options | Budget separately |

Cloudflare Pages currently supports 500 builds per month on its free plan, custom domains and automatic deployment from GitHub. <Cite refs={["turn548371search3","turn815651search4","turn815651search6"]}/>

Supabase offers a free database tier, but free projects can pause after inactivity, so I wouldn't rely on it for a live hospital appointment system. <Cite ref="turn548371search0"/>

### Estimated annual operating costs

| Item | Basic website | Appointment app |
|---|---:|---:|
| Domain | ?800–?1,500 | ?800–?1,500 |
| Hosting | ?0 | ?6,000–?24,000 |
| SSL | ?0 | ?0–?2,000 |
| Backups / monitoring | ?0–?1,000 | ?2,000–?8,000 |
| Maintenance | ?3,000–?6,000 | ?12,000–?30,000 |
| **Estimated annual total** | **?3,800–?8,500** | **?20,800–?66,500** |

These are planning allowances, not provider quotations. Actual costs depend on usage, reliability requirements, taxes and support scope. SMS, WhatsApp API messaging, paid email, and major feature changes are excluded.

For the basic website, **you could charge ?10,000 for development and ?4,000 per year for maintenance**, with the domain billed separately.

## 7. How to deploy the hospital website for free

<box border radius="lg" padding={3} gap={3}>
  <list marker="number" connector="solid" gap={3}>
    <list-item>
      **Create the project locally**

      Use React and Vite in VS Code.

      ```bash
      npm create vite@latest mahavir-demo -- --template react
      cd mahavir-demo
      npm install
      npm run dev
      ```
    </list-item>
    <list-item>
      **Build and test the website**

      Implement responsive pages, navigation, contact links, accessibility and SEO metadata.

      ```bash
      npm run build
      ```
    </list-item>
    <list-item>
      **Push to a private GitHub repository**

      Use a repository such as `mahavir-hospital-demo`. Keep credentials and private information out of Git.
    </list-item>
    <list-item>
      **Connect Cloudflare Pages**

      Open <Link url="https://dash.cloudflare.com/" title="Cloudflare Dashboard"/> ? Workers & Pages ? Create application ? Connect GitHub repository.

      For Vite, use build command `npm run build` and output directory `dist`.
    </list-item>
    <list-item>
      **Publish the demo**

      Cloudflare provides a temporary `pages.dev` address. Use that for your sales presentation.

      Don't present the demo as the hospital's official website.
    </list-item>
    <list-item>
      **Connect the hospital's domain after approval**

      In Cloudflare Pages, open Custom domains and follow the DNS instructions. The hospital should own the domain and hosting account.
    </list-item>
  </list>
</box>

Cloudflare supports GitHub-triggered deployments, so later website updates can go live automatically after approved code is pushed. <Cite ref="turn815651search8"/>

## 9. Security and privacy requirements

This is especially important because the customer is a hospital.

For Phase 1, I recommend **not collecting medical information at all**. Patients can call the hospital directly, and a basic inquiry form can be avoided initially.

If Phase 2 includes appointments or patient details, we must add:

- Explicit privacy notices and appropriate consent or other lawful processing basis
- HTTPS, secure authentication and role-based authorization
- Minimal personal data collection and defined retention periods
- Encrypted storage and protected backups
- Access logs, monitoring and recovery procedures
- Testing and documented incident-response processes
- Clear data ownership and hosting agreements

India's DPDP Act and Rules have phased implementation provisions, so the exact obligations and effective dates should be reviewed before launching a patient-data system. <Cite refs={["turn815651search2","turn815651search10"]}/>

For your first paid project, keeping medical records out of scope is the safer and more manageable choice.

## 10. Complete development and sales timeline

{@body const plan=[{week:"Week 1",title:"Research and demo",tasks:["Confirm hospital identity and existing website","Ask permission before using hospital branding","Build a fictional-content homepage demo","Deploy demo to Cloudflare Pages"]},{week:"Week 2",title:"Client meeting and proposal",tasks:["Show the demo to hospital management","Ask about existing website and patient inquiries","Confirm pages, languages, budget and content ownership","Send a written proposal and payment milestones"]},{week:"Week 3",title:"Development after approval",tasks:["Receive advance payment and approved content","Build responsive pages and contact features","Test mobile, desktop, links and accessibility","Collect client feedback"]},{week:"Week 4",title:"Launch and handover",tasks:["Complete revisions and acceptance testing","Connect hospital-owned domain and hosting","Publish after final approval","Deliver documentation and maintenance agreement"]}]}
{@body const [completed,setCompleted]=DIL.useState({})}
<box border radius="xl" padding={3} gap={3}>
  <row justify="between" align="center">
    **4-week project checklist**
    <text color="secondary" size="xs">{Object.values(completed).filter(Boolean).length}/16 tasks</text>
  </row>
  {#each plan as p,i}
    <box gap={2} key={p.week}>
      <text weight="medium">{p.week} — {p.title}</text>
      {#each p.tasks as task,j}
        <checkbox key={`${i}-${j}`} checked={!!completed[`${i}-${j}`]} onChange={v=>setCompleted(prev=>({...prev,[`${i}-${j}`]:v}))}>{task}</checkbox>
      {/each}
    </box>
    {#if i!==3}<divider color="subtle"/>{/if}
  {/each}
</box>

## 11. Your quotation and profit calculation

Here is an example of how much you could charge for a basic website.

{@body const [price,setPrice]=DIL.useState(10000)}
{@body const [domain,setDomain]=DIL.useState(1000)}
{@body const [other,setOther]=DIL.useState(500)}
{@body const [labor,setLabor]=DIL.useState(30)}
{@body const margin=price-domain-other}
<box border radius="xl" padding={3} gap={3}>
  <box gap={1}>
    <row justify="between"><label>Client development price</label>**?{price.toLocaleString("en-IN")}**</row>
    <slider min={5000} max={30000} step={1000} value={price} onChange={setPrice}/>
  </box>
  <box gap={1}>
    <row justify="between"><label>Domain cost paid by you</label>**?{domain.toLocaleString("en-IN")}**</row>
    <slider min={0} max={2500} step={100} value={domain} onChange={setDomain}/>
  </box>
  <box gap={1}>
    <row justify="between"><label>Other project expenses</label>**?{other.toLocaleString("en-IN")}**</row>
    <slider min={0} max={5000} step={250} value={other} onChange={setOther}/>
  </box>
  <box gap={1}>
    <row justify="between"><label>Your development hours</label>**{labor} hours**</row>
    <slider min={10} max={100} step={5} value={labor} onChange={setLabor}/>
  </box>
  <divider color="subtle"/>
  <grid columns={2} gap={3}>
    <grid-item>
      <text color="secondary" size="xs">Before your labor and taxes</text>
      <title size="xl" color="success">?{margin.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</title>
    </grid-item>
    <grid-item>
      <text color="secondary" size="xs">Effective hourly amount</text>
      <title size="xl">?{Math.round(margin/labor).toLocaleString("en-IN")}</title>
    </grid-item>
  </grid>
  <caption>This is contribution after the selected expenses, not net profit. It excludes your labor cost, taxes, revisions beyond scope and future support.</caption>
</box>

### Recommended payment terms

For a ?10,000 website:

| Milestone | Payment |
|---|---:|
| 40% advance after agreement | ?4,000 |
| 40% after approved development preview | ?4,000 |
| 20% before final handover | ?2,000 |
| **Total** | **?10,000** |

The agreement should specify the number of pages, revisions, content responsibilities, timeline, third-party expenses, ownership, cancellation terms, and what maintenance includes.

## 12. Free add-ons that increase your offer's value

You can include several useful features without significantly increasing your development costs.

| Add-on | Benefit |
|---|---|
| Google Maps directions | Patients find the hospital |
| Click-to-call button | One-tap phone contact |
| WhatsApp contact link | Easy general inquiries |
| Google Business Profile updates | Accurate Search and Maps information |
| Basic SEO | Better page structure and search visibility |
| SSL certificate | Secure website connection |
| Google Search Console | Monitor search indexing |
| Basic web analytics | Measure website traffic |
| QR code for website | Place on brochures or reception desk |

Google Business Profile itself is free, so you can charge for your time setting it up or improving it, but not describe it as a paid Google listing. <Cite ref="turn815651search0"/>

<box border radius="xl" padding={3} gap={2}>
  **Client discovery checklist**
  {#each questions as q,i}
    <checkbox key={i} checked={!!answers[i]} onChange={v=>setAnswers(p=>({...p,[i]:v}))}>{q}</checkbox>

Cloudflare supports GitHub-triggered deployments, so later website updates can go live automatically after approved code is pushed. <Cite ref="turn815651search8"/>

## 9. Security and privacy requirements

This is especially important because the customer is a hospital.

For Phase 1, I recommend **not collecting medical information at all**. Patients can call the hospital directly, and a basic inquiry form can be avoided initially.

If Phase 2 includes appointments or patient details, we must add:

- Explicit privacy notices and appropriate consent or other lawful processing basis
- HTTPS, secure authentication and role-based authorization
- Minimal personal data collection and defined retention periods
- Encrypted storage and protected backups
- Access logs, monitoring and recovery procedures
- Testing and documented incident-response processes
- Clear data ownership and hosting agreements

India's DPDP Act and Rules have phased implementation provisions, so the exact obligations and effective dates should be reviewed before launching a patient-data system. <Cite refs={["turn815651search2","turn815651search10"]}/>

For your first paid project, keeping medical records out of scope is the safer and more manageable choice.



## 2. Hospital research — what we know

<row align="start" gap={3}>
  <box flex="1" gap={1}>
    <title size="lg"><Entity category="business" ref="turn548371business0" value={turn548371business0.name}/></title>

    <text size="sm">Hospital and critical care facility</text>
    <text size="sm">Public business listing available</text>
  </box>
</row>

Public listings and reviews describe the hospital as providing medical and critical-care services, with references to emergency availability and Dr. Darshan Rakhecha. However, its complete department list, appointment process and official website have not been independently confirmed.

**Opportunity:** A dedicated website could make verified hospital information easier for patients to find. But before selling the project, we must ask whether the hospital already has a website, who maintains its Google listing, and how patients currently contact it.

## 3. What will convince the hospital?

Don't open your pitch with "I can build a website." Start with the problems a website might solve.

| Hospital concern | What your solution offers |
|---|---|
| Patients call for basic information | Services, hours and location online |
| New patients struggle to find the hospital | Google Maps and clear directions |
| Staff answer repeated appointment questions | Appointment inquiry form |
| Doctors' availability is unclear | Verified doctor and OPD schedules |
| Hospital information changes | Easy-to-update content |
| Management wants a professional online presence | Mobile-friendly branded website |

We cannot promise a specific increase in patient numbers or Google rankings. We can offer a more accessible, measurable online experience.

For a complete hospital management system involving patient records, billing, clinical workflows or integrations, **don't quote a fixed amount yet**. It requires a separate requirements document, privacy assessment, security architecture and acceptance plan.

## 5. Technology stack

For the first website, use the simplest reliable technology.

| Layer | Recommended technology |
|---|---|
| UI | React + Tailwind CSS |
| Programming | JavaScript |
| Routing | React Router |
| Images | Optimized WebP |
| Maps | Google Maps link or embed |
| Contact | Phone / WhatsApp links |
| Version control | GitHub private repository |
| Hosting | Cloudflare Pages |
| Domain | Hospital-owned `.in` or `.com` |
| HTTPS | Hosting-managed SSL |

**Why no .NET backend initially?** A brochure website doesn't need a server or database. Adding one creates unnecessary cost and maintenance.

For Phase 2, we can use ASP.NET Core Web API, SQL Server or PostgreSQL, and a secure authentication system.

## How we'll find paying clients

For each business, we'll research its website, Google presence, contact information and services. Then we'll identify a specific improvement worth offering.

For example, Shirpur Help Center lists several coaching classes with contact information, including Saraswati Classes, Dnyandeep Classes and Maths Academy. <Cite ref="turn314443search1"/>

Our next task should be to build a **Shirpur Freelance Lead Tracker** containing:

- Business name and category
- Address and publicly listed contact information
- Existing website and social media
- Website status: verified, directory-only, or not found
- Potential website/application requirements
- Estimated project budget
- Contacted, replied, meeting scheduled, or converted

I recommend researching **30–50 businesses across Shirpur**, prioritizing coaching academies, clinics and local service businesses before approaching larger institutions.

This way, you're building a repeatable freelance business rather than relying on one hospital or one Upwork proposal.

### Current Upwork costs

- **Freelancer Basic:** $0/month.
- **Freelancer Plus:** $19.99/month.
- **Additional Connects:** $0.15 each.
- **Service fee:** 0%–15% of contract earnings. <Cite refs={["turn848669search5","turn848669search4"]}/>

Upwork's current pricing page says Basic users can earn up to 10 Connects monthly through qualifying activities; they are not necessarily credited automatically. <Cite ref="turn848669search5"/>

### My recommendation for you

Since you're starting freelancing:

1. Stay on the free **Freelancer Basic** plan.
2. Don't purchase identity verification or Plus unless necessary.
3. Build 2–3 portfolio projects.
4. Use any available Connects on carefully selected jobs.
5. Consider purchasing Connects only when you're ready to submit strong proposals.

**One question:** On your Upwork dashboard, how many Connects does it show — **0, 10, or another number?** I'll guide you on how to start applying without unnecessary spending.

**No, Upwork is not fully paid.** You can create a freelancer account and use its basic features for free.

However, **applying for jobs can cost money**, which is important to understand before you start.

| Upwork feature | Free or paid? |
|--------|--------|
| Create an account | Free |
| Create your freelancer profile | Free |
| Browse freelance jobs | Free |
| Add portfolio projects | Free |
| Submit job proposals | Usually requires Connects |
| Freelancer Plus | Paid, optional |
| Service fee on earnings | Charged when you earn |

### What are Connects?

Connects are Upwork's virtual credits used to apply for jobs. Different jobs require different numbers of Connects.

You don't need a Freelancer Plus subscription to purchase Connects or submit proposals.

### Current Upwork costs

- **Freelancer Basic\:** \$0/month.
- **Freelancer Plus\:** \$19.99/month.
- **Additional Connects\:** \$0.15 each.
- **Service fee\:** 0%–15% of contract earnings. ?cite?turn848669search5?turn848669search4?

Upwork's current pricing page says Basic users can earn up to 10 Connects monthly through qualifying activities; they are not necessarily credited automatically. ?cite?turn848669search5?

### My recommendation for you

Since you're starting freelancing\:

1. Stay on the free **Freelancer Basic** plan.
2. Don't purchase identity verification or Plus unless necessary.
3. Build 2–3 portfolio projects.
4. Use any available Connects on carefully selected jobs.
5. Consider purchasing Connects only when you're ready to submit strong proposals.

**One question\:** On your Upwork dashboard, how many Connects does it show — **0, 10, or another number?** I'll guide you on how to start applying without unnecessary spending.

On the **Upwork website**, you can find identity verification in your account settings.

### Steps on desktop or mobile browser

1. Open ?url?Upwork website?https://www.upwork.com/? and log in.
2. Click your **profile picture/account icon** in the top-right corner. On mobile, open the **? menu** if needed.
3. Select **Account Settings**\.
4. Find **Identity Verification**\.
5. Open it to see your verification status and available options. ?cite?turn768042search0?

### What you may see

| Option | Meaning |
|--------|--------|
| Verify identity | Upwork requires you to verify |
| Spend 35 Connects | Optional identity verification badge |
| Verified | Verification completed |
| No verification request | You may need to wait until Upwork requests it |

Upwork says mandatory verification is initiated through an account notification or email, while optional badge verification can be purchased using 35 Connects. ?cite?turn768042search2?turn768042search3?

**My recommendation\:** Don't purchase Freelancer Plus for this.

On the **Upwork website**, you can find identity verification in your account settings.

### Steps on desktop or mobile browser

1. Open <Link url="https://www.upwork.com/" title="Upwork website"/> and log in.
2. Click your **profile picture/account icon** in the top-right corner. On mobile, open the **? menu** if needed.
3. Select **Account Settings**.
4. Find **Identity Verification**.
5. Open it to see your verification status and available options. <Cite ref="turn768042search0"/>

### What you may see

| Option | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Verify identity | Upwork requires you to verify |
| Spend 35 Connects | Optional identity verification badge |
| Verified | Verification completed |
| No verification request | You may need to wait until Upwork requests it |

Upwork says mandatory verification is initiated through an account notification or email, while optional badge verification can be purchased using 35 Connects. <Cite refs={["turn768042search2","turn768042search3"]}/>

**My recommendation:** Don't purchase Freelancer Plus for this.

Open **Account Settings ? Identity Verification** and send me a screenshot of that page. I'll tell you exactly which option to select.

**No, you do not need an Upwork Plus subscription to complete standard identity verification.** ?

Upwork has two different things\:

| Feature | Subscription required? |
|--------|--------|
| Create freelancer profile | No |
| Standard identity verification when requested by Upwork | No |
| Apply for jobs | No subscription, but Connects may cost money |
| Freelancer Plus subscription | Optional, paid |
| Optional identity verification badge | May require Connects or meeting eligibility conditions |

The **“Get 50% off 1 month of Plus”** offer in your screenshot is an optional subscription promotion, not a requirement for standard verification.

According to Upwork's current help documentation, the optional verification badge costs **35 Connects**, but it is not required for most job applications. ?cite?turn185438search0?turn185438search1?

**My recommendation\:** Don't purchase Plus just for verification.

### How to add them on Upwork

1. Tap **\+ Web Development** from the suggested skills.
2. Search for `ASP.NET Core` in the **Enter skills here** field.
3. Select the matching skill from the dropdown.
4. Repeat for the remaining skills until you've added 15.

**Important\:** Don't select unrelated suggestions like International Development, Screenplay or Integral Calculus.

2. Select **For my personal use** \(or a business option if offered and appropriate).
3. Enter your name and choose your preferred Gmail address.
4. Set a strong password and enable 2-Step Verification.
5. Add your recovery email or phone number.

### How to create your Gmail account

1. Open <Link url="https://accounts.google.com/signup" title="Create Google Account"/>.
2. Select **For my personal use** (or a business option if offered and appropriate).
3. Enter your name and choose your preferred Gmail address.
4. Set a strong password and enable 2-Step Verification.
5. Add your recovery email or phone number.

## 4\. Choose your portfolio technology

Because you already work with .NET and Python, I suggest\:

| Component | Technology |
|--------|--------|
| Frontend | React + Tailwind CSS |
| Backend | ASP.NET Core (for demo projects) |
| AI projects | Python + FastAPI |
| Source control | GitHub |
| Portfolio hosting | Vercel or Netlify |
| Domain | Custom `.dev` or `.com` later |

Your personal portfolio itself can be a static React site. You don't need to build a backend just to display your work.

## 4. Choose your portfolio technology

Because you already work with .NET and Python, I suggest:

| Component | Technology |
|---|---|
| Frontend | React + Tailwind CSS |
| Backend | ASP.NET Core (for demo projects) |
| AI projects | Python + FastAPI |
| Source control | GitHub |
| Portfolio hosting | Vercel or Netlify |
| Domain | Custom `.dev` or `.com` later |

Your personal portfolio itself can be a static React site. You don't need to build a backend just to display your work.

- **Backend Development:** C#, ASP.NET Core, Web API, RESTful Services
- **Database Development:** MS SQL Server, Database Design, Query Optimization
- **Frontend Development:** Web Application Development and Integration
- **Cloud Technologies:** Microsoft Azure, Google Cloud Platform
- **Security:** JWT Authentication, OAuth 2.0, Role-Based Authorization
- **Python:** Automation, Data Processing, and AI/ML Development

**Services I Offer:**
- Custom Web Application Development
- REST API Development and Integration
- Database Design and Optimization
- Backend Bug Fixing and Performance Improvement
- Authentication and Authorization Implementation
- Python Automation and Data Processing

I focus on clean architecture, maintainable code, secure development practices, and delivering reliable solutions that meet business requirements.

Let's discuss how I can help bring your project to life.

Yes. We can build a **professional freelance profile and portfolio** that helps you attract clients, showcase your projects, and start earning through freelance development work.

Since you already have 3+ years of experience as a .NET Full Stack Developer and are expanding into AI/ML, I recommend positioning yourself as a **Full Stack Developer | .NET | Python | AI Integration**.

This lets you offer services using your existing experience while gradually adding AI projects. <MemoryCite />

## 4. Projects to showcase

| Project | Skills demonstrated |
|---|---|
| E-Commerce Backend API | ASP.NET Core, JWT, SQL Server |
| Employee Management System | CRUD, Authentication, Database |
| AI Document Chatbot | Python, RAG, LLM APIs |
| Python Automation Tool | Python, Pandas, File Processing |
| Full Stack Admin Dashboard | Frontend, APIs, Database |

For every completed project, include screenshots, a GitHub repository, a short explanation, the technology stack, and a live demo when possible. Avoid publishing confidential employer code.

## 5. Your 30-day action plan

{@body const tasks = [{week:"Week 1",title:"Freelance identity",items:["Finalize your headline and service offerings","Create Upwork and Fiverr profiles","Prepare a professional profile photo"]},{week:"Week 2",title:"Portfolio foundation",items:["Organize GitHub repositories","Build portfolio website","Publish your first completed project"]},{week:"Week 3",title:"Showcase your work",items:["Complete two additional demo projects","Write project case studies","Publish portfolio website"]},{week:"Week 4",title:"Start client outreach",items:["Create two Fiverr service listings","Send tailored Upwork proposals","Share portfolio on LinkedIn"]}]}
{@body const [done,setDone] = DIL.useState({})}
<box border radius="xl" padding={3} gap={3}>
  <row justify="between" align="center">
    **Freelance launch checklist**
    <text color="secondary" size="xs" tabularNums>{Object.values(done).filter(Boolean).length}/12 completed</text>
  </row>
  <box background="surface-tertiary" height="6px" radius="full" clip>
    <box background="rgba(35,140,100,0.85)" height="100%" width={`${Object.values(done).filter(Boolean).length/12*100}%`} radius="full"/>
  </box>
  {#each tasks as t,i}
    <box gap={2} key={t.week}>
      <text weight="medium">{t.week} — {t.title}</text>
      {#each t.items as item,j}
        <checkbox key={`${i}-${j}`} checked={!!done[`${i}-${j}`]} onChange={v=>setDone(p=>({...p,[`${i}-${j}`]:v}))}>{item}</checkbox>
      {/each}
    </box>
    {#if i!==3}<divider color="subtle"/>{/if}
  {/each}
</box>

## 6. First, choose your freelance direction

{@body const [focus,setFocus] = DIL.useState("both")}
{@body const [site,setSite] = DIL.useState("both")}
<box border radius="xl" padding={3} gap={3}>
  <form gap={3} onSubmit={()=>GenUI.issueNewTurn(`Help me build my freelance profile and portfolio step by step. My freelance focus is ${focus}. I want ${site === "both" ? "both a portfolio website and marketplace profiles" : site === "website" ? "a personal portfolio website" : "freelance marketplace profiles"}. Start with the first concrete step and give me ready-to-use content.`)}>
    <box gap={2}>
      <text weight="medium" size="sm">What type of freelance work do you want?</text>
      <radio-group direction="col" value={focus} onChange={setFocus}>
        <radio value="dotnet">.NET Full Stack Development</radio>
        <radio value="ai">Python, AI and ML Development</radio>
        <radio value="both">Both .NET and AI/Python (recommended)</radio>
      </radio-group>
    </box>
    <divider color="subtle"/>
    <box gap={2}>
      <text weight="medium" size="sm">What should we build first?</text>
      <radio-group direction="col" value={site} onChange={setSite}>
        <radio value="website">Personal portfolio website</radio>
        <radio value="marketplace">Upwork and Fiverr profiles</radio>
        <radio value="both">Both, step by step</radio>
      </radio-group>
    </box>
    <button block submit>Start building my freelance profile <icon name="arrow-right" inline/></button>
  </form>
</box>
